    [propget] VT_HRESULT Preferences([out, retval] IRobotPreferences** ret);
    [propget] VT_HRESULT Window([out, retval] IRobotWindow** ret);
    VT_HRESULT Quit([in] IRobotQuitOption quit_option);
    [propget] VT_HRESULT CmpntFactory([out, retval] IRobotComponentFactory** ret);
    [propget] VT_HRESULT Kernel([out, retval] IRobotKernel** ret);
    [propget] VT_HRESULT ProgramVersion([out, retval] VT_BSTR* ret);
    [propget] VT_HRESULT UserControl([out, retval] VT_BOOL* ret);
    [propput] VT_HRESULT UserControl([in] VT_BOOL _);
};

    const VT_INT I_QO_DISCARD_CHANGES = 0;
    const VT_INT I_QO_PROMPT_TO_SAVE_CHANGES = 1;
    const VT_INT I_QO_SAVE_CHANGES = 2;
} IRobotQuitOption;
